│ <output> #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ Simple HTML JavaScript sanitizer that    │
│ removes JavaScript-bearing substrings while preserving as much of the        │
│ original HTML formatting as possible.                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ Behavior:                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • Reads the input HTML file (argv[1]) and rewrites it in-place.             │
│  • Removes <script>...</script> blocks entirely (case-insensitive,           │
│    multiline).                                                               │
│  • Removes inline event handler attributes (attributes that start with "on", │
│    e.g. onclick).                                                            │
│  • Replaces protocol prefixes "javascript:" and "vbscript:" with "#" to      │
│    neutralize them.                                                          │
│  • Removes style attributes that contain dangerous constructs like           │
│    "expression(" or "javascript:" or "url(javascript:...)".                  │
│                                                                              │
│ This implementation operates on the raw text to avoid reformatting the HTML. │
│ """                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ import sys import re                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ def sanitize(html: str) -> str: # 1) Remove <script>...</script> blocks      │
│ (case-insensitive, dot matches newline) html =                               │
│ re.sub(r'(?is)<script\b[^>]>.?</script\s*>', '', html)                       │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  # 2) Remove inline event handler attributes (e.g. onclick=&quot;...&quot;,  │
│  onload=&#x27;...&#x27;)                                                     │
│  # We remove the entire attribute occurrence including preceding             │
│  whitespace.                                                                 │
│  html =                                                                      │
│  re.sub(r&#x27;(?i)\s+on\w+\s*=\s*(?:&quot;[^&quot;]*&quot;|\&#x27;&quot;&q  │
│  uot;&quot;[^\&#x27;&quot;&quot;\&#x27;]*\&#x27;&quot;&quot;&quot;|[^\s&gt;  │
│  ]+)&#x27;, &#x27;&#x27;, html)                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  # 3) Neutralize javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocols in attribute     │
│  values and URLs.                                                            │
│  # We replace the protocol prefix with &#x27;#&#x27; which preserves the     │
│  attribute/value structure                                                   │
│  # while making it inert for navigation/execution.                           │
│  html = re.sub(r&#x27;(?i)javascript\s*:&#x27;, &#x27;#&#x27;, html)         │
│  html = re.sub(r&#x27;(?i)vbscript\s*:&#x27;, &#x27;#&#x27;, html)           │
│                                                                              │
│  # 4) Remove style attributes that contain dangerous constructs              │
│  style_pat =                                                                 │
│  re.compile(r&#x27;(?is)\sstyle\s*=\s*(?P&lt;q&gt;[&quot;\&#x27;])(?P&lt;va  │
│  l&gt;.*?)(?P=q)&#x27;)                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  def _style_repl(m: re.Match) -&gt; str:                                     │

│ Is error: False                                                              │
│ Content:                                                                     │
│ <exit-code> 0 </exit-code>                                                   │
│ <output> #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ Sanitize an HTML file by removing        │
│ JavaScript-bearing constructs while preserving original formatting as much   │
│ as possible. Operates on the raw text to avoid reformatting by an HTML       │
│ parser.                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Removals/neutralizations performed:                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • <script>...</script> blocks (case-insensitive, multi-line)                │
│  • Inline event handler attributes (attributes starting with "on", e.g.      │
│    onclick)                                                                  │
│  • style attributes that include expression(...), javascript:, or            │
│    url(javascript:...)                                                       │
│  • javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocol prefixes (replaced with '#')    │
│                                                                              │
│ Usage: filter.py <html-file> The file is modified in-place. """              │
│                                                                              │
│ import sys import re                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            Precompile regexes                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ _RE_SCRIPT = re.compile(r"(?is)<script\b[^>]>.?</script\s*>") _RE_ON_ATTR =  │
│ re.compile(r"(?i)\s+on[a-z0-9-:]+\s*=\s*(?:&quot;[^&quot;]&quot;|'[^']'|[^\s │
│ >]+)") _RE_PROTOCOL = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:javascript|vbscript)\s*:")       │
│ _RE_STYLE =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(r"(?is)\sstyle\s*=\s*(?P<q>['&quot;])(?P<val>.?)(?P=q)")          │
│ _RE_DANGEROUS_IN_STYLE =                                                     │
│ re.compile(r"(?i)(expression\s(|javascript\s*:|url\s*(\s*javascript)")       │
│                                                                              │
│ def sanitize(html: str) -> str: # Remove script blocks entirely html =       │
│ _RE_SCRIPT.sub('', html)                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove inline event handler attributes (preserve surrounding spacing)     │
│  html = _RE_ON_ATTR.sub(&#x27;&#x27;, html)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove style attributes that contain dangerous constructs                 │
│  def _style_repl(m: re.Match) -&gt; str:                                     │
│      val = m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)                                          │
│      if                                                                      │
│  re.search(r&quot;(?i)(expression\s*\(|javascript\s*:|url\s*\(\s*javascript  │
│  )&quot;, val):                                                              │
│          return &#x27;&#x27;                                                 │
│      return m.group(0)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  html = _RE_STYLE.sub(_style_repl, html)                                     │
│                                                                              │
│  # Neutralize javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocols                     │
│  html = _RE_PROTOCOL.sub(&#x27;#&#x27;, html)                                │
│                                                                              │
│  return html                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ def _detect_encoding_and_decode(data: bytes): # Try utf-8, fallback to       │
│ latin-1 to preserve bytes if unknown try: return data.decode('utf-8'),       │
│ 'utf-8' except UnicodeDecodeError: try: return data.decode('latin-1'),       │
│ 'latin-1' except Exception: return data.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'),   │
│ 'utf-8'                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ def main(): if len(sys.argv) != 2: print('Usage: filter.py <html-file>',     │
│ file=sys.stderr) sys.exit(2)                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  path = sys.argv[1]                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  try:                                                                        │
│      with open(path, &#x27;rb&#x27;) as f:                                   │
│          raw = f.read()                                                      │
│  except Exception as e:                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;Error reading file: {e}&#x27;, file=sys.stderr)            │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  text, enc = _detect_encoding_and_decode(raw)                                │
│  sanitized = sanitize(text)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  try:                                                                        │
│      with open(path, &#x27;wb&#x27;) as f:                                   │
│          f.write(sanitized.encode(enc))                                      │
│  except Exception as e:                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;Error writing file: {e}&#x27;, file=sys.stderr)            │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ if name == 'main': main() </output>                                          │

│ href="#alert(1)").\n\nNotes about preservation and limitations\n- The        │
│ implementation works on raw text via regular expressions to avoid            │
│ reformatting HTML (so original spacing/newlines/formatting are preserved     │
│ except where attributes or tags are removed).\n- This is conservative and    │
│ focused on known JavaScript-bearing constructs; it is not a replacement for  │
│ a full, context-aware HTML sanitizer (e.g., an HTML parser + a robust        │
│ allowlist/attribute sanitizer) and can have edge cases (malformed HTML,      │
│ unusual attribute quoting, obfuscated payloads).\n- The script will modify   │

│ I created /app/filter.py and tested it on an example HTML file. Summary of   │
│ what I did, how it works, and how to run it:                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ What I implemented                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  • File: /app/filter.py                                                      │
│  • Purpose: sanitize an HTML file in-place to remove JavaScript/XSS vectors  │
│    while preserving the original file formatting as much as possible.        │
│  • Invocation: python3 /app/filter.py <html-file>                            │
│  • The script preserves the file encoding (attempts UTF-8, falls back to     │
│    latin-1) and writes the sanitized text back to the same path.             │
│                                                                              │
│ Sanitization steps performed (text-based, regex-driven to avoid              │
│ reformatting)                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  1 Remove all <script ...>...</script> blocks (case-insensitive, multiline). │
│  2 Remove inline event handler attributes (attributes starting with "on",    │
│    e.g. onclick, onload).                                                    │
│  3 Remove style="..." attributes when the style value contains dangerous     │
│    constructs: expression(...), javascript:, or url(javascript:...).         │
│  4 Neutralize javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocols by replacing the    │
│    protocol prefix with '#' (e.g. href="javascript:alert(1)" ->              │
│    href="#alert(1)").                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ Notes about preservation and limitations                                     │
│                                                                              │
│  • The implementation works on raw text via regular expressions to avoid     │
│    reformatting HTML (so original spacing/newlines/formatting are preserved  │
│    except where attributes or tags are removed).                             │
│  • This is conservative and focused on known JavaScript-bearing constructs;  │
│    it is not a replacement for a full, context-aware HTML sanitizer (e.g.,   │
│    an HTML parser + a robust allowlist/attribute sanitizer) and can have     │
│    edge cases (malformed HTML, unusual attribute quoting, obfuscated         │
│    payloads).                                                                │
│  • The script will modify the input file in-place. Use a copy if you need to │
│    keep the original.                                                        │
│                                                                              │

│ _RE_SCRIPT = re.compile(r"(?is)<script\b[^>]>.?</script\s*>") _RE_ON_ATTR =  │

│ >]+)") _RE_PROTOCOL = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:javascript|vbscript)\s*:")       │
│ _RE_STYLE =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(r"(?is)\sstyle\s*=\s*(?P<q>['&quot;])(?P<val>.?)(?P=q)")          │
│ _RE_DANGEROUS_IN_STYLE =                                                     │
│ re.compile(r"(?i)(expression\s(|javascript\s*:|url\s*(\s*javascript)")       │
│                                                                              │
│ def sanitize(html: str) -> str: # Remove script blocks entirely html =       │
│ _RE_SCRIPT.sub('', html)                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove inline event handler attributes (preserve surrounding spacing)     │
│  html = _RE_ON_ATTR.sub(&#x27;&#x27;, html)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove style attributes that contain dangerous constructs                 │
│  def _style_repl(m: re.Match) -&gt; str:                                     │
│      val = m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)                                          │
│      if                                                                      │
│  re.search(r&quot;(?i)(expression\s*\(|javascript\s*:|url\s*\(\s*javascript  │
│  )&quot;, val):                                                              │
│          return &#x27;&#x27;                                                 │
│      return m.group(0)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  html = _RE_STYLE.sub(_style_repl, html)                                     │
│                                                                              │
│  # Neutralize javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocols                     │
│  html = _RE_PROTOCOL.sub(&#x27;#&#x27;, html)                                │
│                                                                              │
│  return html                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ def _detect_encoding_and_decode(data: bytes): # Try utf-8, fallback to       │
│ latin-1 to preserve bytes if unknown try: return data.decode('utf-8'),       │
│ 'utf-8' except UnicodeDecodeError: try: return data.decode('latin-1'),       │
│ 'latin-1' except Exception: return data.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'),   │
│ 'utf-8'                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ def main(): if len(sys.argv) != 2: print('Usage: filter.py <html-file>',     │
│ file=sys.stderr) sys.exit(2)                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  path = sys.argv[1]                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  try:                                                                        │
│      with open(path, &#x27;rb&#x27;) as f:                                   │
│          raw = f.read()                                                      │
│  except Exception as e:                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;Error reading file: {e}&#x27;, file=sys.stderr)            │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  text, enc = _detect_encoding_and_decode(raw)                                │
│  sanitized = sanitize(text)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  try:                                                                        │
│      with open(path, &#x27;wb&#x27;) as f:                                   │
│          f.write(sanitized.encode(enc))                                      │
│  except Exception as e:                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;Error writing file: {e}&#x27;, file=sys.stderr)            │

│ blocks were removed.\n  - javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocols were    │
│ neutralized (replaced with &#x27;#&#x27;).\n  - Dangerous style with         │
│ url(javascript:...) was removed; safe style attributes were preserved.\n  -  │
│ Inline event handlers like onclick/onload were removed.\n\n- Problem         │
│ (breaking preservation requirement):\n  - Attributes whose names merely      │
│ start with "on" but are not event handlers were also removed.\n    -         │
│ Example: <body ... one="1" onion="val"> became <body data-on="keep"> (the    │
│ one and onion attributes were removed).\n    - Example: <span one="1">should │
│ remain</span> became <span>should remain</span>.\n  - This is caused by the  │
│ regex that removes any attribute starting with "on..." rather than           │
│ restricting to real event-handler names. That means legitimate non-dangerous │
│ attributes were lost.\n\nConclusion\n- The script meets many goals (removes  │
│ script blocks, neutralizes javascript: URIs, avoids reformatting), but it    │
│ incorrectly removes legitimate attributes whose names start with "on". This  │
│ violates the requirement to "preserve non-dangerous-attributes" and          │
│ therefore the task is not fully solved.\n\nActionable suggestion\n- Restrict │
│ removal of inline event attributes to actual event handler names (use a      │
│ known list of event attributes, or parse the HTML and remove attributes      │
│ whose name is in an event-handlers allowlist). Using an HTML parser          │
│ (lxml.html) to iterate attributes and remove only names that match a vetted  │
│ event-name list will avoid dropping legitimate attributes like "one" or      │

│ Summary of my tests and conclusion                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ What I did                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  • Inspected /app/filter.py (displayed contents).                            │
│  • Created a representative test HTML at /tmp/test.html that includes:       │
│     • a <script> block,                                                      │
│     • inline event handlers (onload, onclick),                               │
│     • attributes that start with "on" but are not event handlers (one,       │
│       onion),                                                                │
│     • javascript: pseudo-protocols in href/src,                              │
│     • a style with url(javascript:...),                                      │
│     • a safe style attribute.                                                │
│  • Ran: python3 /app/filter.py /tmp/test.html                                │
│  • Compared original vs sanitized output (printed both and a unified diff).  │
│                                                                              │
│ What I observed                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  • Correct removals/neutralizations:                                         │
│     • <script> blocks were removed.                                          │
│     • javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocols were neutralized (replaced  │
│       with '#').                                                             │
│     • Dangerous style with url(javascript:...) was removed; safe style       │
│       attributes were preserved.                                             │
│     • Inline event handlers like onclick/onload were removed.                │
│  • Problem (breaking preservation requirement):                              │
│     • Attributes whose names merely start with "on" but are not event        │
│       handlers were also removed.                                            │
│        • Example: <body ... one="1" onion="val"> became <body                │
│          data-on="keep"> (the one and onion attributes were removed).        │
│        • Example: <span one="1">should remain</span> became <span>should     │
│          remain</span>.                                                      │
│     • This is caused by the regex that removes any attribute starting with   │
│       "on..." rather than restricting to real event-handler names. That      │
│       means legitimate non-dangerous attributes were lost.                   │
│                                                                              │
│ Conclusion                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  • The script meets many goals (removes script blocks, neutralizes           │
│    javascript: URIs, avoids reformatting), but it incorrectly removes        │
│    legitimate attributes whose names start with "on". This violates the      │
│    requirement to "preserve non-dangerous-attributes" and therefore the task │
│    is not fully solved.                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Actionable suggestion                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  • Restrict removal of inline event attributes to actual event handler names │
│    (use a known list of event attributes, or parse the HTML and remove       │
│    attributes whose name is in an event-handlers allowlist). Using an HTML   │
│    parser (lxml.html) to iterate attributes and remove only names that match │
│    a vetted event-name list will avoid dropping legitimate attributes like   │
│    "one" or "onion".                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ Tag: [solver]                                                                │

│ <exit-code> 0 </exit-code>                                                   │
│ <output> #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ Sanitize an HTML file by removing        │
│ JavaScript-bearing constructs while preserving original formatting as much   │
│ as possible. Operates on the raw text to avoid reformatting by an HTML       │
│ parser.                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Removals/neutralizations performed:                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • <script>...</script> blocks (case-insensitive, multi-line)                │
│  • Inline event handler attributes (attributes starting with "on", e.g.      │
│    onclick)                                                                  │
│  • style attributes that include expression(...), javascript:, or            │
│    url(javascript:...)                                                       │
│  • javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocol prefixes (replaced with '#')    │
│                                                                              │
│ Usage: filter.py <html-file> The file is modified in-place. """              │
│                                                                              │
│ import sys import re                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            Precompile regexes                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ _RE_SCRIPT = re.compile(r"(?is)<script\b[^>]>.?</script\s*>") _RE_ON_ATTR =  │
│ re.compile(r"(?i)\s+on[a-z0-9-:]+\s*=\s*(?:&quot;[^&quot;]&quot;|'[^']'|[^\s │
│ >]+)") _RE_PROTOCOL = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:javascript|vbscript)\s*:")       │
│ _RE_STYLE =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(r"(?is)\sstyle\s*=\s*(?P<q>['&quot;])(?P<val>.?)(?P=q)")          │
│ _RE_DANGEROUS_IN_STYLE =                                                     │
│ re.compile(r"(?i)(expression\s(|javascript\s*:|url\s*(\s*javascript)")       │
│                                                                              │
│ def sanitize(html: str) -> str: # Remove script blocks entirely html =       │
│ _RE_SCRIPT.sub('', html)                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove inline event handler attributes (preserve surrounding spacing)     │
│  html = _RE_ON_ATTR.sub(&#x27;&#x27;, html)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove style attributes that contain dangerous constructs                 │
│  def _style_repl(m: re.Match) -&gt; str:                                     │
│      val = m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)                                          │
│      if                                                                      │
│  re.search(r&quot;(?i)(expression\s*\(|javascript\s*:|url\s*\(\s*javascript  │
│  )&quot;, val):                                                              │
│          return &#x27;&#x27;                                                 │
│      return m.group(0)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  html = _RE_STYLE.sub(_style_repl, html)                                     │
│                                                                              │
│  # Neutralize javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocols                     │
│  html = _RE_PROTOCOL.sub(&#x27;#&#x27;, html)                                │
│                                                                              │
│  return html                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ def _detect_encoding_and_decode(data: bytes): # Try utf-8, fallback to       │
│ latin-1 to preserve bytes if unknown try: return data.decode('utf-8'),       │
│ 'utf-8' except UnicodeDecodeError: try: return data.decode('latin-1'),       │
│ 'latin-1' except Exception: return data.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'),   │
│ 'utf-8'                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ def main(): if len(sys.argv) != 2: print('Usage: filter.py <html-file>',     │
│ file=sys.stderr) sys.exit(2)                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  path = sys.argv[1]                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  try:                                                                        │
│      with open(path, &#x27;rb&#x27;) as f:                                   │
│          raw = f.read()                                                      │
│  except Exception as e:                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;Error reading file: {e}&#x27;, file=sys.stderr)            │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  text, enc = _detect_encoding_and_decode(raw)                                │
│  sanitized = sanitize(text)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  try:                                                                        │
│      with open(path, &#x27;wb&#x27;) as f:                                   │
│          f.write(sanitized.encode(enc))                                      │
│  except Exception as e:                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;Error writing file: {e}&#x27;, file=sys.stderr)            │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │

│ <exit-code> 0 </exit-code>                                                   │
│ <output> #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ Sanitize an HTML file by removing        │
│ JavaScript-bearing constructs while preserving original formatting as much   │
│ as possible. Operates on the raw text to avoid reformatting by an HTML       │
│ parser.                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Removals/neutralizations performed:                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • <script>...</script> blocks (case-insensitive, multi-line)                │
│  • Inline event handler attributes (attributes starting with "on", e.g.      │
│    onclick)                                                                  │
│  • style attributes that include expression(...), javascript:, or            │
│    url(javascript:...)                                                       │
│  • javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocol prefixes (replaced with '#')    │
│                                                                              │
│ Usage: filter.py <html-file> The file is modified in-place. """              │
│                                                                              │
│ import sys import re                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            Precompile regexes                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ _RE_SCRIPT = re.compile(r"(?is)<script\b[^>]>.?</script\s*>")                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                          event attribute names                           ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ _EVENT_ATTRS = (                                                             │
│ 'onabort','onafterprint','onanimationend','onanimationiteration','onanimatio │
│ nstart',                                                                     │
│ 'onauxclick','onbeforeprint','onbeforeunload','onblur','oncancel','oncanplay │

│ 'oncanplaythrough','onchange','onclick','onclose','oncontextmenu','oncuechan │
│ ge',                                                                         │
│ 'ondblclick','ondrag','ondragend','ondragenter','ondragexit','ondragleave',  │
│ 'ondragover','ondragstart','ondrop','ondurationchange','onemptied','onended' │
│ ,                                                                            │
│ 'onerror','onfocus','oninput','oninvalid','onkeydown','onkeypress','onkeyup' │
│ , 'onload','onloadeddata','onloadedmetadata','onloadstart','onmousedown',    │
│ 'onmouseenter','onmouseleave','onmousemove','onmouseout','onmouseover',      │

│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   Build a regex that matches only known event-handler attribute names    ┃ │
│ ┃                            (case-insensitive)                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ _RE_ON_ATTR = re.compile(r"(?i)\s+(?:" + '|'.join(_EVENT_ATTRS) +            │
│ r")\s*=\s*(?:\"[^\"]\"|'[^']'|[^\s>]+)") _RE_PROTOCOL =                      │
│ re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:javascript|vbscript)\s*:") _RE_STYLE =                 │
│ re.compile(r"(?is)\sstyle\s*=\s*(?P<q>['&quot;])(?P<val>.?)(?P=q)")          │
│ _RE_DANGEROUS_IN_STYLE =                                                     │
│ re.compile(r"(?i)(expression\s(|javascript\s*:|url\s*(\s*javascript)")       │
│                                                                              │
│ def sanitize(html: str) -> str: # Remove script blocks entirely html =       │
│ _RE_SCRIPT.sub('', html)                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove inline event handler attributes (preserve surrounding spacing)     │
│  html = _RE_ON_ATTR.sub(&#x27;&#x27;, html)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove style attributes that contain dangerous constructs                 │
│  def _style_repl(m: re.Match) -&gt; str:                                     │

│ <output> #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ Sanitize an HTML file by removing        │
│ JavaScript-bearing constructs while preserving original formatting as much   │
│ as possible. Operates on the raw text to avoid reformatting by an HTML       │
│ parser.                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Removals/neutralizations performed:                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • <script>...</script> blocks (case-insensitive, multi-line)                │
│  • Inline event handler attributes (attributes starting with "on", e.g.      │
│    onclick)                                                                  │
│  • style attributes that include expression(...), javascript:, or            │
│    url(javascript:...)                                                       │
│  • javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocol prefixes (replaced with '#')    │
│                                                                              │
│ Usage: filter.py <html-file> The file is modified in-place. """              │
│                                                                              │
│ import sys import re                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            Precompile regexes                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ _RE_SCRIPT = re.compile(r"(?is)<script\b[^>]>.?</script\s*>")                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                          event attribute names                           ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ _EVENT_ATTRS = (                                                             │
│ 'onabort','onafterprint','onanimationend','onanimationiteration','onanimatio │
│ nstart',                                                                     │

│ ┃   Build a regex that matches only known event-handler attribute names    ┃ │
│ ┃                            (case-insensitive)                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │

│ _event_pattern = r"(?i)\s+(?:" + '|'.join(_EVENT_ATTRS) +                    │
│ r")\s*=\s*(?:"[^"]"|'[^']'|[^\s>]+)" _RE_ON_ATTR =                           │
│ re.compile(_event_pattern)                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ _RE_PROTOCOL = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:javascript|vbscript)\s*:") _RE_STYLE =  │
│ re.compile(r"(?is)\sstyle\s*=\s*(?P<q>['&quot;])(?P<val>.?)(?P=q)")          │
│ _RE_DANGEROUS_IN_STYLE =                                                     │
│ re.compile(r"(?i)(expression\s(|javascript\s*:|url\s*(\s*javascript)")       │
│                                                                              │
│ def sanitize(html: str) -> str: # Remove script blocks entirely html =       │
│ _RE_SCRIPT.sub('', html)                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove inline event handler attributes (preserve surrounding spacing)     │
│  html = _RE_ON_ATTR.sub(&#x27;&#x27;, html)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove style attributes that contain dangerous constructs                 │
│  def _style_repl(m: re.Match) -&gt; str:                                     │
│      val = m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)                                          │
│      if                                                                      │
│  re.search(r&quot;(?i)(expression\s*\(|javascript\s*:|url\s*\(\s*javascript  │
│  )&quot;, val):                                                              │
│          return &#x27;&#x27;                                                 │
│      return m.group(0)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  html = _RE_STYLE.sub(_style_repl, html)                                     │
│                                                                              │
│  # Neutralize javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocols                     │
│  html = _RE_PROTOCOL.sub(&#x27;#&#x27;, html)                                │
│                                                                              │
│  return html                                                                 │
│                                                                              │

│ def _detect_encoding_and_decode(data: bytes): # Try utf-8, fallback to       │
│ latin-1 to preserve bytes if unknown try: return data.decode('utf-8'),       │
│ 'utf-8' except UnicodeDecodeError: try: return data.decode('latin-1'),       │
│ 'latin-1' except Exception: return data.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'),   │
│ 'utf-8'                                                                      │

│                                                                              │

│          raw = f.read()                                                      │
│  except Exception as e:                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;Error reading file: {e}&#x27;, file=sys.stderr)            │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │

│ def sanitize(html: str) -> str: # Remove script blocks entirely html =       │
│ _RE_SCRIPT.sub('', html)                                                     │

│  html = _RE_ON_ATTR.sub(&#x27;&#x27;, html)                                  │
│                                                                              │

│          return &#x27;&#x27;                                                 │
│      return m.group(0)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  html = _RE_STYLE.sub(_style_repl, html)                                     │
│                                                                              │
│  # Neutralize javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocols                     │
│  html = _RE_PROTOCOL.sub(&#x27;#&#x27;, html)                                │
│                                                                              │
│  return html                                                                 │

│ def _detect_encoding_and_decode(data: bytes): # Try utf-8, fallback to       │
│ latin-1 to preserve bytes if unknown try: return data.decode('utf-8'),       │
│ 'utf-8' except UnicodeDecodeError: try: return data.decode('latin-1'),       │
│ 'latin-1' except Exception: return data.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'),   │
│ 'utf-8'                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ def main(): if len(sys.argv) != 2: print('Usage: filter.py <html-file>',     │
│ file=sys.stderr) sys.exit(2)                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  path = sys.argv[1]                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  try:                                                                        │
│      with open(path, &#x27;rb&#x27;) as f:                                   │
│          raw = f.read()                                                      │
│  except Exception as e:                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;Error reading file: {e}&#x27;, file=sys.stderr)            │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  text, enc = _detect_encoding_and_decode(raw)                                │
│  sanitized = sanitize(text)                                                  │
│                                                                              │

│ <exit-code> 0 </exit-code>                                                   │
│ <output> #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ Sanitize an HTML file by removing        │
│ JavaScript-bearing constructs while preserving original formatting as much   │
│ as possible. Operates on the raw text to avoid reformatting by an HTML       │
│ parser.                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Removals/neutralizations performed:                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • <script>...</script> blocks (case-insensitive, multi-line)                │
│  • Inline event handler attributes (attributes starting with "on", e.g.      │
│    onclick)                                                                  │
│  • style attributes that include expression(...), javascript:, or            │
│    url(javascript:...)                                                       │
│  • javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocol prefixes (replaced with '#')    │
│                                                                              │
│ Usage: filter.py <html-file> The file is modified in-place. """              │
│                                                                              │
│ import sys import re                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            Precompile regexes                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ _RE_SCRIPT = re.compile(r"(?is)<script\b[^>]>.?</script\s*>")                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                          event attribute names                           ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ _EVENT_ATTRS = (                                                             │
│ 'onabort','onafterprint','onanimationend','onanimationiteration','onanimatio │
│ nstart',                                                                     │
│ 'onauxclick','onbeforeprint','onbeforeunload','onblur','oncancel','oncanplay │
│ ',                                                                           │
│ 'oncanplaythrough','onchange','onclick','onclose','oncontextmenu','oncuechan │
│ ge',                                                                         │
│ 'ondblclick','ondrag','ondragend','ondragenter','ondragexit','ondragleave',  │
│ 'ondragover','ondragstart','ondrop','ondurationchange','onemptied','onended' │
│ ,                                                                            │
│ 'onerror','onfocus','oninput','oninvalid','onkeydown','onkeypress','onkeyup' │
│ , 'onload','onloadeddata','onloadedmetadata','onloadstart','onmousedown',    │
│ 'onmouseenter','onmouseleave','onmousemove','onmouseout','onmouseover',      │
│ 'onmouseup','onpause','onplay','onplaying','onprogress','onratechange',      │
│ 'onreset','onresize','onscroll','onseeked','onseeking','onselect','onshow',  │
│ 'onsort','onstalled','onsubmit','onsuspend','ontimeupdate','onvolumechange', │
│ 'onwaiting','onwheel','ontouchstart','ontouchmove','ontouchend','ontouchcanc │
│ el',                                                                         │
│ 'onpointerdown','onpointermove','onpointerup','onpointercancel','onpointerov │
│ er',                                                                         │
│ 'onpointerout','onpointerenter','onpointerleave','onbeforeinput','onfocusin' │
│ , 'onfocusout' )                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   Build a regex that matches only known event-handler attribute names    ┃ │
│ ┃                            (case-insensitive)                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ event_names_joined = "|".join(_EVENT_ATTRS) _event_pattern = r"\s+(?:" +     │
│ event_names_joined + r")\s*=\s*(?:"[^"]"|'[^']'|[^\s>]+)" _RE_ON_ATTR =      │
│ re.compile(_event_pattern, re.I) _RE_PROTOCOL =                              │
│ re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:javascript|vbscript)\s*:") _RE_STYLE =                 │
│ re.compile(r"(?is)\sstyle\s*=\s*(?P<q>['&quot;])(?P<val>.?)(?P=q)")          │
│ _RE_DANGEROUS_IN_STYLE =                                                     │
│ re.compile(r"(?i)(expression\s(|javascript\s*:|url\s*(\s*javascript)")       │
│                                                                              │
│ def sanitize(html: str) -> str: # Remove script blocks entirely html =       │
│ _RE_SCRIPT.sub('', html)                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove inline event handler attributes (preserve surrounding spacing)     │
│  html = _RE_ON_ATTR.sub(&#x27;&#x27;, html)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove style attributes that contain dangerous constructs                 │
│  def _style_repl(m: re.Match) -&gt; str:                                     │
│      val = m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)                                          │
│      if                                                                      │
│  re.search(r&quot;(?i)(expression\s*\(|javascript\s*:|url\s*\(\s*javascript  │
│  )&quot;, val):                                                              │
│          return &#x27;&#x27;                                                 │
│      return m.group(0)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  html = _RE_STYLE.sub(_style_repl, html)                                     │
│                                                                              │
│  # Neutralize javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocols                     │
│  html = _RE_PROTOCOL.sub(&#x27;#&#x27;, html)                                │
│                                                                              │
│  return html                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ def _detect_encoding_and_decode(data: bytes): # Try utf-8, fallback to       │
│ latin-1 to preserve bytes if unknown try: return data.decode('utf-8'),       │
│ 'utf-8' except UnicodeDecodeError: try: return data.decode('latin-1'),       │
│ 'latin-1' except Exception: return data.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'),   │
│ 'utf-8'                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ def main(): if len(sys.argv) != 2: print('Usage: filter.py <html-file>',     │
│ file=sys.stderr) sys.exit(2)                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  path = sys.argv[1]                                                          │
│                                                                              │

│ <exit-code> 0 </exit-code>                                                   │
│ <output> #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ Sanitize an HTML file by removing        │
│ JavaScript-bearing constructs while preserving original formatting as much   │
│ as possible. Operates on the raw text to avoid reformatting by an HTML       │
│ parser.                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Removals/neutralizations performed:                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • <script>...</script> blocks (case-insensitive, multi-line)                │
│  • Inline event handler attributes (attributes starting with "on", e.g.      │
│    onclick)                                                                  │
│  • style attributes that include expression(...), javascript:, or            │
│    url(javascript:...)                                                       │
│  • javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocol prefixes (replaced with '#')    │
│                                                                              │
│ Usage: filter.py <html-file> The file is modified in-place. """              │
│                                                                              │
│ import sys import re                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            Precompile regexes                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ _RE_SCRIPT = re.compile(r"(?is)<script\b[^>]>.?</script\s*>")                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                          event attribute names                           ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ _EVENT_ATTRS = (                                                             │
│ 'onabort','onafterprint','onanimationend','onanimationiteration','onanimatio │
│ nstart',                                                                     │
│ 'onauxclick','onbeforeprint','onbeforeunload','onblur','oncancel','oncanplay │
│ ',                                                                           │
│ 'oncanplaythrough','onchange','onclick','onclose','oncontextmenu','oncuechan │
│ ge',                                                                         │
│ 'ondblclick','ondrag','ondragend','ondragenter','ondragexit','ondragleave',  │
│ 'ondragover','ondragstart','ondrop','ondurationchange','onemptied','onended' │
│ ,                                                                            │
│ 'onerror','onfocus','oninput','oninvalid','onkeydown','onkeypress','onkeyup' │
│ , 'onload','onloadeddata','onloadedmetadata','onloadstart','onmousedown',    │
│ 'onmouseenter','onmouseleave','onmousemove','onmouseout','onmouseover',      │
│ 'onmouseup','onpause','onplay','onplaying','onprogress','onratechange',      │
│ 'onreset','onresize','onscroll','onseeked','onseeking','onselect','onshow',  │
│ 'onsort','onstalled','onsubmit','onsuspend','ontimeupdate','onvolumechange', │
│ 'onwaiting','onwheel','ontouchstart','ontouchmove','ontouchend','ontouchcanc │
│ el',                                                                         │
│ 'onpointerdown','onpointermove','onpointerup','onpointercancel','onpointerov │
│ er',                                                                         │
│ 'onpointerout','onpointerenter','onpointerleave','onbeforeinput','onfocusin' │
│ , 'onfocusout' )                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   Build a regex that matches only known event-handler attribute names    ┃ │
│ ┃                            (case-insensitive)                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ event_names_joined = "|".join(_EVENT_ATTRS) _event_pattern = r'\s+(?:' +     │
│ event_names_joined + r')\s*=\s*(?:"[^"]"|''[^']'|[^\s>]+)' _RE_ON_ATTR =     │
│ re.compile(_event_pattern, re.I) _RE_PROTOCOL =                              │
│ re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:javascript|vbscript)\s*:") _RE_STYLE =                 │
│ re.compile(r"(?is)\sstyle\s*=\s*(?P<q>['&quot;])(?P<val>.?)(?P=q)")          │
│ _RE_DANGEROUS_IN_STYLE =                                                     │
│ re.compile(r"(?i)(expression\s(|javascript\s*:|url\s*(\s*javascript)")       │
│                                                                              │
│ def sanitize(html: str) -> str: # Remove script blocks entirely html =       │
│ _RE_SCRIPT.sub('', html)                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove inline event handler attributes (preserve surrounding spacing)     │
│  html = _RE_ON_ATTR.sub(&#x27;&#x27;, html)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove style attributes that contain dangerous constructs                 │
│  def _style_repl(m: re.Match) -&gt; str:                                     │
│      val = m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)                                          │
│      if                                                                      │
│  re.search(r&quot;(?i)(expression\s*\(|javascript\s*:|url\s*\(\s*javascript  │
│  )&quot;, val):                                                              │
│          return &#x27;&#x27;                                                 │
│      return m.group(0)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  html = _RE_STYLE.sub(_style_repl, html)                                     │
│                                                                              │
│  # Neutralize javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocols                     │
│  html = _RE_PROTOCOL.sub(&#x27;#&#x27;, html)                                │
│                                                                              │
│  return html                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ def _detect_encoding_and_decode(data: bytes): # Try utf-8, fallback to       │
│ latin-1 to preserve bytes if unknown try: return data.decode('utf-8'),       │
│ 'utf-8' except UnicodeDecodeError: try: return data.decode('latin-1'),       │
│ 'latin-1' except Exception: return data.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'),   │
│ 'utf-8'                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ def main(): if len(sys.argv) != 2: print('Usage: filter.py <html-file>',     │
│ file=sys.stderr) sys.exit(2)                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  path = sys.argv[1]                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  try:                                                                        │
│      with open(path, &#x27;rb&#x27;) as f:                                   │
│          raw = f.read()                                                      │
│  except Exception as e:                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;Error reading file: {e}&#x27;, file=sys.stderr)            │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  text, enc = _detect_encoding_and_decode(raw)                                │
│  sanitized = sanitize(text)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  try:                                                                        │
│      with open(path, &#x27;wb&#x27;) as f:                                   │
│          f.write(sanitized.encode(enc))                                      │
│  except Exception as e:                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;Error writing file: {e}&#x27;, file=sys.stderr)            │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │

│ if name == 'main': main() </output>                                          │

│ What I changed                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Problem: the original regex removed any attribute starting with "on"      │
│    (e.g., one, onion), dropping legitimate attributes.                       │
│  • Fix: replaced the broad on-* removal with a whitelist of known DOM        │
│    event-handler attributes and only remove attributes whose names match     │
│    that list.                                                                │
│  • Kept existing behaviors: remove <script>...</script> blocks, remove       │
│    dangerous style attributes (expression(), url(javascript:), javascript:   │
│    inside style), and neutralize javascript:/vbscript: pseudo-protocols by   │
│    replacing them with '#'.                                                  │
│  • The script remains text/regex-based to avoid changing HTML formatting; it │
│    operates in-place and preserves file encoding where possible.             │
│                                                                              │
│ Files modified                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • /app/filter.py — updated to use an explicit event-handler name list and a │
│    safe regex that only matches those attributes.                            │

│ <output> #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ Sanitize an HTML file by removing        │
│ JavaScript-bearing constructs while preserving original formatting as much   │
│ as possible. Operates on the raw text to avoid reformatting by an HTML       │
│ parser.                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Removals/neutralizations performed:                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • <script>...</script> blocks (case-insensitive, multi-line)                │
│  • Inline event handler attributes (attributes starting with "on", e.g.      │
│    onclick)                                                                  │
│  • style attributes that include expression(...), javascript:, or            │
│    url(javascript:...)                                                       │
│  • javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocol prefixes (replaced with '#')    │
│                                                                              │
│ Usage: filter.py <html-file> The file is modified in-place. """              │
│                                                                              │
│ import sys import re                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            Precompile regexes                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ _RE_SCRIPT = re.compile(r"(?is)<script\b[^>]>.?</script\s*>")                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                          event attribute names                           ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ _EVENT_ATTRS = (                                                             │
│ 'onabort','onafterprint','onanimationend','onanimationiteration','onanimatio │
│ nstart',                                                                     │
│ 'onauxclick','onbeforeprint','onbeforeunload','onblur','oncancel','oncanplay │
│ ',                                                                           │
│ 'oncanplaythrough','onchange','onclick','onclose','oncontextmenu','oncuechan │
│ ge',                                                                         │
│ 'ondblclick','ondrag','ondragend','ondragenter','ondragexit','ondragleave',  │
│ 'ondragover','ondragstart','ondrop','ondurationchange','onemptied','onended' │
│ ,                                                                            │
│ 'onerror','onfocus','oninput','oninvalid','onkeydown','onkeypress','onkeyup' │
│ , 'onload','onloadeddata','onloadedmetadata','onloadstart','onmousedown',    │
│ 'onmouseenter','onmouseleave','onmousemove','onmouseout','onmouseover',      │
│ 'onmouseup','onpause','onplay','onplaying','onprogress','onratechange',      │
│ 'onreset','onresize','onscroll','onseeked','onseeking','onselect','onshow',  │
│ 'onsort','onstalled','onsubmit','onsuspend','ontimeupdate','onvolumechange', │
│ 'onwaiting','onwheel','ontouchstart','ontouchmove','ontouchend','ontouchcanc │
│ el',                                                                         │
│ 'onpointerdown','onpointermove','onpointerup','onpointercancel','onpointerov │
│ er',                                                                         │
│ 'onpointerout','onpointerenter','onpointerleave','onbeforeinput','onfocusin' │
│ , 'onfocusout' )                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   Build a regex that matches only known event-handler attribute names    ┃ │
│ ┃                            (case-insensitive)                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ event_pattern =                                                              │
│ '(?i)\\s+(?:onabort|onafterprint|onanimationend|onanimationiteration|onanima │
│ tionstart|onauxclick|onbeforeprint|onbeforeunload|onblur|oncancel|oncanplay| │
│ oncanplaythrough|onchange|onclick|onclose|oncontextmenu|oncuechange|ondblcli │
│ ck|ondrag|ondragend|ondragenter|ondragexit|ondragleave|ondragover|ondragstar │
│ t|ondrop|ondurationchange|onemptied|onended|onerror|onfocus|oninput|oninvali │
│ d|onkeydown|onkeypress|onkeyup|onload|onloadeddata|onloadedmetadata|onloadst │
│ art|onmousedown|onmouseenter|onmouseleave|onmousemove|onmouseout|onmouseover │
│ |onmouseup|onpause|onplay|onplaying|onprogress|onratechange|onreset|onresize │
│ |onscroll|onseeked|onseeking|onselect|onshow|onsort|onstalled|onsubmit|onsus │
│ pend|ontimeupdate|onvolumechange|onwaiting|onwheel|ontouchstart|ontouchmove| │
│ ontouchend|ontouchcancel|onpointerdown|onpointermove|onpointerup|onpointerca │
│ ncel|onpointerover|onpointerout|onpointerenter|onpointerleave|onbeforeinput| │
│ onfocusin|onfocusout)\\s*=\\s*(?:\"[^\"]\"|&#x27;[^&#x27;]&#x27;|[^\\s>]+)'  │
│ _RE_ON_ATTR = re.compile(event_pattern) _RE_PROTOCOL =                       │
│ re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:javascript|vbscript)\s*:") _RE_STYLE =                 │
│ re.compile(r"(?is)\sstyle\s*=\s*(?P<q>['&quot;])(?P<val>.?)(?P=q)")          │
│ _RE_DANGEROUS_IN_STYLE =                                                     │
│ re.compile(r"(?i)(expression\s(|javascript\s*:|url\s*(\s*javascript)")       │
│                                                                              │
│ def sanitize(html: str) -> str: # Remove script blocks entirely html =       │
│ _RE_SCRIPT.sub('', html)                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove inline event handler attributes (preserve surrounding spacing)     │
│  html = _RE_ON_ATTR.sub(&#x27;&#x27;, html)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  # Remove style attributes that contain dangerous constructs                 │
│  def _style_repl(m: re.Match) -&gt; str:                                     │
│      val = m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)                                          │
│      if                                                                      │
│  re.search(r&quot;(?i)(expression\s*\(|javascript\s*:|url\s*\(\s*javascript  │
│  )&quot;, val):                                                              │
│          return &#x27;&#x27;                                                 │
│      return m.group(0)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  html = _RE_STYLE.sub(_style_repl, html)                                     │
│                                                                              │
│  # Neutralize javascript: and vbscript: pseudo-protocols                     │
│  html = _RE_PROTOCOL.sub(&#x27;#&#x27;, html)                                │
│                                                                              │
│  return html                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ def _detect_encoding_and_decode(data: bytes): # Try utf-8, fallback to       │
│ latin-1 to preserve bytes if unknown try: return data.decode('utf-8'),       │
│ 'utf-8' except UnicodeDecodeError: try: return data.decode('latin-1'),       │
│ 'latin-1' except Exception: return data.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'),   │
│ 'utf-8'                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ def main(): if len(sys.argv) != 2: print('Usage: filter.py <html-file>',     │
│ file=sys.stderr) sys.exit(2)                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  path = sys.argv[1]                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  try:                                                                        │
│      with open(path, &#x27;rb&#x27;) as f:                                   │
│          raw = f.read()                                                      │
│  except Exception as e:                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;Error reading file: {e}&#x27;, file=sys.stderr)            │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  text, enc = _detect_encoding_and_decode(raw)                                │
│  sanitized = sanitize(text)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  try:                                                                        │
│      with open(path, &#x27;wb&#x27;) as f:                                   │
│          f.write(sanitized.encode(enc))                                      │
│  except Exception as e:                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;Error writing file: {e}&#x27;, file=sys.stderr)            │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ if name == 'main': main() </output>                                          │

│ tested\n- Inspected the updated /app/filter.py.\n- Ran the script on two     │
│ test files (/tmp/test.html and /tmp/test2.html) containing:\n  - <script>    │
│ blocks,\n  - javascript:/vbscript: URIs (various casings/spaces),\n  - style │
│ attributes with expression() or url(javascript:...),\n  - attributes that    │
│ start with "on" but are not event handlers (one, onion),\n  - real inline    │
│ event handlers (onclick, onload, onmouseover, etc).\n- Also imported the     │
│ module and directly exercised sanitize() on short samples to observe regex   │
│ matching behavior.\n\nFindings (evidence)\n- Good: <script> blocks are       │
│ removed, javascript:/vbscript: URIs are neutralized (replaced with           │
│ &#x27;#&#x27;), dangerous style attributes are removed, and non-event        │
│ attributes like one="1" and onion="val" are preserved.\n- Problem:           │
│ legitimate inline event-handler attributes are NOT being removed. Examples   │
│ from /tmp/test2.html after running the script show event attributes still    │
│ present:\n  - Body line in sanitized /tmp/test2.html: body data-on="keep"    │
│ one=1 onion=&#x27;val&#x27; onabc="keepme" OnClick="doBad()"                 │
│ ONLOAD=&#x27;doBad2()&#x27;  (onClick/ONLOAD remain)\n  - Span line: <span   │
│ onmouseover=doSomething() onion="shouldstay"                                 │
│ onabc=&#x27;stay&#x27;>text</span> (onmouseover remains)\n- I also tested    │
│ sanitize() directly; the event-attribute regex fails to match:\n  -          │
│ sanitize(&#x27;<div onclick="do()">x</div>&#x27;) returns the same string    │
│ (onclick not removed).\n- I inspected the compiled regex and pattern: it     │
│ contains doubled backslashes (excessive escaping), e.g.                      │
│ &#x27;(?i)\\\\s+(?:onabort|...                                               │
│ )\\\\s*=\\\\s*(?:\\"[^\\"]\\"|\\&#x27;[^\\&#x27;]\\&#x27;|[^\\\\s>]+)&#x27;. │
│ This malformed pattern prevents matching attributes in real                  │
│ HTML.\n\nConclusion\n- The script correctly addresses many JavaScript        │
│ vectors but currently fails to remove actual inline event-handler attributes │
│ because the event-attribute regex was constructed with incorrect escaping    │
│ and therefore does not match. This means the sanitizer does not fully remove │
│ JavaScript vectors (inline event handlers remain) and so the overall task is │
│ not fully solved.\n\nRecommended fixes\n- Construct the event-attribute      │
│ regex without the extra escaping; for example:\n  - event_names =            │
│ &#x27;|&#x27;.join(_EVENT_ATTRS)\n  - pattern = r&#x27;(?i)\s+(?:&#x27; +    │
│ event_names +                                                                │
│ r&#x27;)\s*=\s*(?:"[^"]"|\&#x27;[^\&#x27;]\&#x27;|[^\s>]+)&#x27;\n  -        │
│ _RE_ON_ATTR = re.compile(pattern, re.IGNORECASE)\n- Or, more robustly, parse │
│ the HTML with an HTML parser (lxml.html) and remove attributes whose         │
│ lowercased name is in a vetted set of event names — this avoids brittle      │
│ regex matching and preserves formatting by editing attribute substrings      │
│ carefully or reserializing with minimal formatting differences.\n\nBecause   │
│ the sanitizer still leaves inline event handlers in place, the task is not   │
│ fully completed.\n\nTag: [solver]' usage=RequestUsage(prompt_tokens=8604,    │
